| IMPL-06 | 2026-10-07 | Mobile uses Expo SDK 57 with exactly the versions the SDK specifies (React 19.2.3, React Native 0.86.3, TypeScript ~6.0), which differ from the web app (React 19.3.0, TS 5.9). The three native variants use distinct identities `pk.punjablearningportal.student{.dev,.preview,}`. These IDs are **PROVISIONAL** until the owner settles the product name (B11) and must be final before the first store submission. | Engineering (Expo docs; roadmap §5.5) | A preview or production config refuses to build without an explicit `PORTAL_API_ORIGIN` (checked in CI). |
| IMPL-05 (rev. 2026-10-07) | 2026-10-07 | **Not-found/error semantics, measured on Next 16.4.0 with `cacheComponents: true`, `partialPrefetching: true`:** (1) URL matching no route (`/no-such-page`, `/learn/11/biology/extra/segment`) → **HTTP 404**, not-found page, `noindex`. (2) Route matches but the catalogue record doesn't exist or is out of scope (`/learn/11/english`, an unknown chapter UUID, a malformed chapter ID) → `notFound()` runs inside the streamed Suspense boundary after the shell, so **HTTP 200** + not-found page + `noindex`. This is documented framework behaviour (`not-found.md` "Calling notFound() after streaming has started"; `dynamicParams` is rejected under Cache Components). (3) Retired catalogue entry → API **410** → the web shows the same not-found page with `noindex`. (4) API unreachable or 5xx → **HTTP 200** shell, the server logs `ApiUnavailableError`, and the client error boundary shows "We couldn’t load this page" with a reference and **Try again**. No experimental dependency was added to change (2)-(4). | Engineering | E2E asserts (1) status 404 and (2) noindex + not-found UI. |
| IMPL-04 | 2026-10-06 | Stable IDs for curriculum entities are deterministic UUIDv5 values derived from a natural key (`region/grade/subject/book-edition/chapter/topic`), with chapter numbers stored only as display attributes. | Engineering (roadmap §5.2 "use stable IDs; slugs and chapter numbers can change") | Re-importing an unchanged book yields identical IDs; an edition change yields new IDs and needs an explicit mapping. |
| IMPL-03 | 2026-10-06 | Monorepo: `apps/api` (FastAPI, uv), `apps/web` (Next.js), `apps/mobile` (Expo), `packages/contracts` (generated OpenAPI types), `content/` (derived source registry and catalogue), `infra/` (local services). | Engineering (P03.S1.T1) | Single CI workflow with path filters. |
| IMPL-02 | 2026-10-06 | The stack is pinned at P01.S1.T1 from versions verified on 2026-10-06 (see `docs/project/STACK.md`). Prefer supported stable lines over the newest major where compatibility is unproven. For example, TypeScript 7 (native compiler) is deferred until Next.js/ESLint support is verified. | Engineering (roadmap §5.0) | Upgrades follow the §6.4 requalification rule. |
| IMPL-01 | 2026-10-06 | The GitHub repository is **public**, so the owner's textbook PDFs and full-text OCR layers are **not committed**. They stay in the local `11 Class Data/` and `12 Class Data/` folders, are registered by SHA-256 in `content/source_registry.json`, and are regenerable locally. Navigation indexes (headings, page refs, short captions, counts) are committed. | Engineering (copyright: books print "All rights reserved"; roadmap §5.6 originals unchanged) | CI cannot read the PDFs. Ingestion runs locally/in a private environment. **Recommendation to owner: make the repository private** (then this can be revisited). |
| SCOPE-01 | 2026-10-06 | **Owner-approved active academic scope:** Punjab, Pakistan; Class XI and Class XII kept separate; subjects Biology, Chemistry, Physics, Computer Science and Mathematics ("Maths" is an alias). English, Logical Reasoning, Statistics, compulsory board subjects and other provinces are **outside the currently authorized academic scope**. Schemas stay extensible to them. | Owner (implementation command, 2026-10-06) | Recorded in D01/launch manifest. A full official-pattern MDCAT/ECAT mock requiring English/Logical Reasoning/Statistics stays **unavailable** with an accurate explanation; subject/section practice for the five subjects is allowed. A combined XI+XII revision is an explicit same-subject test mode with traceable per-grade questions. |
| D01 (interim) | 2026-10-06 | Launch manifest scope = SCOPE-01 with all requested test modes for the five subjects and required R2 chapter short/long written scope. Full-mock availability is per profile, subject to SCOPE-01. | Owner (scope) + roadmap default | See `docs/project/LAUNCH_MANIFEST.md`. G22 closes the final manifest. |
| AUTH-01 | 2026-10-06 | Standing implementation authorization: edit, install compatible dependencies, migrate, test, commit/push, PR and merge through required checks, deploy through configured environments when release conditions hold; use existing funded resources within limits. It does **not** authorize new spending, contacting people, charging customers, public launch, or bypassing protections/reviews. | Owner | Routine phase confirmations are no longer requested. Genuine external dependencies are recorded in BLOCKERS.md. |

## Section 17 decisions (all at proposed default unless listed above)
D02–D14 remain at the roadmap's proposed defaults. Nothing has been purchased, contracted or enrolled. See BLOCKERS.md for the external inputs each one waits on.


## Written-assessment owner decisions WA-D01 to WA-D15 (W00.S3.T2; register written 2026-10-12)

These are roadmap §20.19's decisions. **None has an owner or lead approval on record.** The "interim default" column
is what the code does today, each provisional and reversible, so engineering could continue. An approval is recorded
here with its date and approver. A blocker is never an approval.

| ID | Decision | Roadmap recommendation (§20.19) | Interim default in the code | Approval | Needs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| WA-D01 | First subject, grade, chapter and language | One well-supported Class XI conceptual short-answer slice from owner data | None chosen. Engineering uses technical fixture questions only. | Not recorded | B01 (owner material, reviewers) |
| WA-D02 | Rubric authority and reviewer team | Official schemes where available, otherwise teacher-reviewed practice rubrics with independent approval | `official_scheme` needs a published reference, otherwise `practice_rubric`; independent review and publisher separation enforced (IMPL-19, RUBRIC-PATHS-01) | Not recorded | B01 (named reviewers) |
| WA-D03 | Writing, upload and optional-answer policy | Separate D/G/U; proposed 10/20-minute allowances only after byte and uplink qualification | D, G and U are separate and pinned; G defaults to 10 minutes (IMPL-20); no optional-answer choices | Not recorded | Device qualification (B09, B14) |
| WA-D04 | Input formats and page limits | Qualified JPEG/PNG/PDF and the §20.8 caps | JPEG, PNG and PDF with byte, pixel, side and page caps (EVIDENCE-01); HEIC converted to JPEG on the phone (NATIVE-W01) | Not recorded | Device qualification (B14) |
| WA-D05 | Model/OCR route and terms | Benchmark permitted candidates on real scripts; pin versions or teacher review | No provider: the assessor is off by default and the fixture is refused in staging and production (AUTOASSESS-01); unexpected model versions are rejected (AUTO-PROVENANCE-01) | Not recorded | B10 |
| WA-D06 | Statistical release policy | Freeze severe-error definitions and thresholds before the holdout | Not frozen. Only a fixed-share evaluation subset exists (CALIBRATION-01). | Not recorded | B10 (real data), evaluation lead |
| WA-D07 | Trial and paid allowance and rates | Cost-backed weighted units, original bucket and period, remedies, no new trial | Weights short 1, long 2 units (IMPL-22), not cost-backed; the cohort economics report can price them once rates exist (ECONOMICS-01) | Not recorded | Owner, B06 |
| WA-D08 | Review staffing and service | Fund real coverage; subtract fixed and backlog work before admission | Operator-entered staffing figures and admission limits (STAFFING-01, ALLOC-01, ADMIT-01) | Not recorded | Real staffing (B01) |
| WA-D09 | Rescan, appeal and remedy windows | Proposed 14-day recheck, 7-day action and remedy, three-way rescan classification, mixed completeness | 14-day recheck (RECHECK-01); 7-day learner actions (RESCAN-01); three-way classification (RESCAN-02); completeness per §20.9.3 (COMPLETENESS-01) | Not recorded | Academic + product leads |
| WA-D10 | Evidence and score retention and deletion | Proposed 90-day scans and readings, 12-month feedback, bounded appeal holds | Nothing is erased automatically; deletion requests are routed to support (PRIVACY-01) | Not recorded | B15 |
| WA-D11 | Urdu, numericals and diagrams | Qualify each slice independently or keep it unavailable | Not offered: English text answers only | Not recorded | B01, B10 |
| WA-D12 | Release scope and load promise | R2 chapter short and long scope for the default complete product | R2 is required for the complete-product claim (SCOPE-01, launch manifest); nothing beyond is claimed; deployed roles run the R0 pilot unless `PORTAL_WRITTEN_RELEASE` says otherwise (PILOT-R0-01) | Not recorded | Owner |
| WA-D13 | Progressive upload and capture policy | Upload from the start; a measured 2,000-3,000-pixel preset with a higher-detail fallback | Uploads open from the start; the capture policy version is pinned per form; no measured preset | Not recorded | B09, B14 |
| WA-D14 | Provider processing location and terms | Approve each candidate before any real-script call | None approved, so no real-script call is possible | Not recorded | B10 |
| WA-D15 | Composite board-paper policy | Only source-verified parent/child timing, choices, maxima, corrections and release | Not built: chapter-only written practice, clearly labelled | Not recorded | B02, owner |
